2f:["$","ul",null,{"children":["\n",["$","li",null,{"children":["\n",["$","p",null,{"children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"It can tackle problems with more binary variables than the QPU has qubits."}]," Presolve applies reduction rules that can fix variables and simplify the remaining QUBO while preserving at least one optimal solution.
